Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in magnetometry theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 144 and the invariant I(A,B) = 669 mod 3.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the magnetometry decomposition. By minimality, the element a^144 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 669-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
